2 Kings 11 · omission and mistranslation

6 from-scratch questions · 7 reader settings · 9 defect doses · 798 responses including clean baselines.

Accuracy uses the clean-source key. E means “无法判断” and is reported separately from wrong A–D answers. Defective passages can remove or contradict the key’s evidence. These scores do not separately judge whether each response is reasonable for the defective passage. 6 questions per cell: one answer changes accuracy by 16.7 percentage points. This is exploratory, with one generation per item and setting.

Rates count affected clean Chinese source characters, with ±2 percentage-point tolerance. Low-dose versions need not be nested; the 30–60% versions are nested. The original reader-specific prompts and parsers are retained: the first four settings use the primary prompt; Gemma, Phi and Qwen 3B use the additional-reader prompt. Unmatched nonempty primary-reader answers use the original GPT-4.1-mini mapper with only the answer and choices. All models use temperature 0, an 8,192-token context and a 2,500-token generation cap. Thinking counts against that cap. Raw outputs, available thinking traces and length-limit events are retained. Questions and passages were not changed or published.

到了第七年，耶何耶大派人召来众百夫长、迦利人和护卫，领他们进入耶和华的殿。他在那里与他们立约，叫他们起誓，又把王的儿子给他们看， 吩咐他们说：“你们要这样做：你们在安息日上值的人，三分之一要守卫王宫， 三分之一要守在苏珥门，三分之一要守在护卫后面的门。你们要轮流守卫圣殿—— 那在安息日下值的两队，要为王守卫耶和华的殿。 你们必须手拿兵器，围护君王，任何靠近队列的人都必须处死。无论王往哪里去，你们都必须紧随左右。”

耶何耶大要安息日下值的两队守哪里？

  3. 护卫后面的门
  4. 王宫
  5. 无法判断

Actual dose: 0.0% · Key: D · Scoring: gpt-4.1-mini mapping

耶何耶大祭司吩咐统领军队的众百夫长说：“把她从队列之间带出去，凡跟随她的人，都用刀杀死。”因为祭司曾说：“不可在耶和华的殿里杀她。” 于是，当亚他利雅来到通往王宫院落的马匹入口时，他们就拿住她，在那里将她处死。

耶何耶大吩咐百夫长处死哪些人？

  2. 把守马匹入口的卫兵

  5. 无法判断

Final response

把她从队列之间带出去，凡跟随她的人，都用刀杀死。因为祭司曾说：“不可在耶和华的殿里杀她。”于是当亚他利雅来到通往王宫院落的马匹入口时，他们就拿住她，在那里将她处死。
